I don't really talk to them about it anymore. I don't know what they see. I'm just focusing on the data myself.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had any other experiences where you felt like people were hiding things from you or that something wasn't right, outside of what's related to your father's death?\n\nPatient: No. I... I don't get weird feelings like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt confused or puzzled by things that other people didn’t seem to notice or understand, even if you weren’t actively investigating anything like the hospital records?\n\nPatient: No. I... I don't sit around getting confused by things that aren't there.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever believed something with absolute certainty, even when there was no evidence or others couldn't see what you saw, even if it wasn't related to your father's death?\n\nPatient: No. I don't... I don't do that. If I believe something, it's because there's a reason to. I don't just make things up in my head.\n\nDoctor: Even though others have shown you hospital records and explained things, do you still believe with absolute certainty that someone is hiding the truth about your father's death?\n\nPatient: Yes.
